工作目录 ~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld。泰拉瑞亚1.4.5.6复刻项目,反编译源码标杆在 Terarria1456/。

背景:我们的地下沙漠(underground desert)腔体比原版"开阔"——实测:沙漠墙(187砂岩墙/216硬化沙墙)空气腔里,琥珀宝石簇均 3.9 格/簇,原版金标只有 1.5 格/簇;说明原版地下沙漠内部多是窄缝/小洞(腔内空气格基本都贴着实心沙岩柱),我们的是大空洞。

任务:
1. 精读原版地下沙漠生成:Terarria1456/Terraria.GameContent.Biomes/DesertBiome.cs 的 Place 全流程,以及它引用的 Terraria.GameContent.Biomes.Desert/ 目录(DesertHive.cs、DesertDescription.cs 等,可能还有 Chambers/RockFormations/Anthill/LarvaHole 等子类——find 全目录)。重点输出:**腔体是怎么挖出来的**——每个 chamber/洞的尺寸参数、随机走挖(类似 TileRunner?)、岩石柱子怎么留、墙(187/216)在哪些格上涂、有没有"空气格必须贴墙/贴柱"的隐含结构。
2. 精读复刻版 game/src/world/gen/vanilla/DesertPass.ts 全文(714行,含 hive/anthill/larva/pit),对照原版逐段找偏差,重点找"我们挖得比原版大/洞更连通"的位置:比如 chamber 半径/步数/随机游走参数抄错、墙涂的范围比原版大、柱子间距留得太开、或者某个填充(沙岩柱)段缺失。
3. 特别注意任何 while/拒绝采样循环:列出复刻版 DesertPass.ts 里所有 while 循环+行号+是否有硬上界(防死循环审查,历史事故:liquidType+1 编码照抄==0 必死循环)。
4. 输出:原版腔体雕刻关键代码段(文件:行号)、复刻版对应段(文件:行号)、具体偏差清单(按影响大小排序)、建议修法。不要改任何代码。
```

1	using Microsoft.Xna.Framework;
2	using Newtonsoft.Json;
3	using Terraria.GameContent.Biomes.Desert;
4	using Terraria.WorldBuilding;
5	
6	namespace Terraria.GameContent.Biomes;
7	
8	public class DesertBiome : MicroBiome
9	{
10		[JsonProperty("ChanceOfEntrance")]
11		public double ChanceOfEntrance = 0.3333;
12	
13		public override bool Place(Point origin, StructureMap structures, GenerationProgress progress)
14		{
15			DesertDescription desertDescription = DesertDescription.CreateFromPlacement(origin);
16			if (!desertDescription.IsValid)
17			{
18				return false;
19			}
20			ExportDescriptionToEngine(desertDescription);
21			SandMound.Place(desertDescription, progress, 0f, 0.1f);
22			desertDescription.UpdateSurfaceMap();
23			if (!Main.tenthAnniversaryWorld && GenBase._random.NextDouble() <= ChanceOfEntrance && !WorldGen.SecretSeed.extraLiquid.Enabled)
24			{
25				switch (GenBase._random.Next(4))
26				{
27				case 0:
28					ChambersEntrance.Place(desertDescription, progress, 0.1f, 0.2f);
29					break;
30				case 1:
31					AnthillEntrance.Place(desertDescription, progress, 0.1f, 0.2f);
32					break;
33				case 2:
34					LarvaHoleEntrance.Place(desertDescription, progress, 0.1f, 0.2f);
35					break;
36				case 3:
37					PitEntrance.Place(desertDescription, progress, 0.1f, 0.2f);
38					break;
39				}
40			}
41			DesertHive.Place(desertDescription, progress, 0.2f, 0.75f);
42			CleanupArea(desertDescription.Hive, progress, 0.75f, 1f);
43			Rectangle area = new Rectangle(desertDescription.CombinedArea.X, 50, desertDescription.CombinedArea.Width, desertDescription.CombinedArea.Bottom - 20);
44			structures.AddStructure(area, 10);
45			return true;
46		}
47	
48		private static void ExportDescriptionToEngine(DesertDescription description)
49		{
50			GenVars.UndergroundDesertLocation = description.CombinedArea;
51			GenVars.UndergroundDesertLocation.Inflate(10, 10);
52			GenVars.UndergroundDesertHiveLocation = description.Hive;
53		}
54	
55		private static void CleanupArea(Rectangle area, GenerationProgress progress, float progressMin, float progressMax)
56		{
57			int num = 20 - area.Left;
58			int num2 = num + area.Right + 20;
59			for (int i = -20 + area.Left; i < area.Right + 20; i++)
60			{
61				progress.Set((float)(i + num) / (float)num2, progressMin, progressMax);
62				for (int j = -20 + area.Top; j < area.Bottom + 20; j++)
63				{
64					if (i > 0 && i < Main.maxTilesX - 1 && j > 0 && j < Main.maxTilesY - 1)
65					{
66						WorldGen.SquareWallFrame(i, j);
67						WorldUtils.TileFrame(i, j, frameNeighbors: true);
68					}
69				}
70			}
71		}
72	}
73

7	
8	namespace Terraria.GameContent.Biomes.Desert;
9	
10	public static class DesertHive
11	{
12		private struct Block(double x, double y)
13		{
14			public Vector2D Position = new Vector2D(x, y);
15		}
16	
17		private class Cluster : List<Block>
18		{
19		}
20	
21		private class ClusterGroup : List<Cluster>
22		{
23			public readonly int Width;
24	
25			public readonly int Height;
26	
27			private ClusterGroup(int width, int height)
28			{
29				Width = width;
30				Height = height;
31				Generate();
32			}
33	
34			public static ClusterGroup FromDescription(DesertDescription description)
35			{
36				return new ClusterGroup(description.BlockColumnCount, description.BlockRowCount);
37			}
38	
39			private static void SearchForCluster(bool[,] blockMap, List<Point> pointCluster, int x, int y, int level = 2)
40			{
41				pointCluster.Add(new Point(x, y));
42				blockMap[x, y] = false;
43				level--;
44				if (level != -1)
45				{
46					if (x > 0 && blockMap[x - 1, y])
47					{
48						SearchForCluster(blockMap, pointCluster, x - 1, y, level);
49					}
50					if (x < blockMap.GetLength(0) - 1 && blockMap[x + 1, y])
51					{
52						SearchForCluster(blockMap, pointCluster, x + 1, y, level);
53					}
54					if (y > 0 && blockMap[x, y - 1])
55					{
56						SearchForCluster(blockMap, pointCluster, x, y - 1, level);
57					}
58					if (y < blockMap.GetLength(1) - 1 && blockMap[x, y + 1])
59					{
60						SearchForCluster(blockMap, pointCluster, x, y + 1, level);
61					}
62				}
63			}
64	
65			private static void AttemptClaim(int x, int y, int[,] clusterIndexMap, List<List<Point>> pointClusters, int index)
66			{
67				int num = clusterIndexMap[x, y];
68				if (num == -1 || num == index)

This is the key insight. The vanilla DesertHive is NOT a random walk at all! It's a **Worley-like / cluster-based noise** approach:

1. Generate a `bool[Width, Height]` block map (`BlockColumnCount` x `BlockRowCount`) where `Width = (maxTilesX/4200)*80` (~80 for small worlds, and BlockScale = (4,2)). For each cell in an ellipse, `array[j,i] = genRand.Next(2)==0` — random 50% fill.

2. Then flood-fill cluster search with depth limit `level=2` (so clusters are small connected components within a 5x5-ish neighborhood search depth 2 → each cluster has at most... actually the depth-limited BFS means cluster merging is very local). Clusters with Count > 2 kept.

3. Then `AttemptClaim` - randomly merges adjacent clusters with 50% chance (either -1 or index).

4. Clusters with < 4 points cleared.

5. Each block point gets jitter ±0.25.

6. Then per-tile: compute block-space coordinate, for each cluster compute `sum(1/distSquared)` metaball field. The field values `num7 = num + num3` (top1 + top2 cluster field sums) decide:
   - `num7 > 3.5`: cavity! tile.ClearEverything(), wall=187 (sandstone brick wall 187), and `if (num2 % 15 == 2) tile.ResetToType(404)` — 404 is... probably "sandstone column pillar"! Actually 404 might be a decorative thing. Let me check tile 404. In Terraria 404 = ??? Actually 396 = Sandstone (砂岩), 397 = Hardened Sand (硬化沙), 53 = Sand, 404 = ??? 404 is probably the new 1.4.4 "Sandstone Column"? Hmm. 485 = antlion egg? 484 = small rock, 165 = cactus? no 165 is cactus. Let me check.
   - `1.8 < num7 <= 3.5`: wall=187, tile type 396 (sandstone), smooth — this is the solid sandstone around the cavity.
   - `0.7 < num7 <= 1.8` or !flag(inside ellipse factor): wall=216 (hardened sand wall), tile = type (53 or 397) — hardened sand outer shell.
   - `0.25 < num7 <= 0.7`: probabilistic transition.
   - else: nothing.

KEY: The cavity is where the metaball field > 3.5. `num6 += 1/distSquared`. For a single isolated block at distance d, field = 1/d². Two blocks... The threshold 3.5 requires being very close to a cluster of blocks. A cluster of ~4 blocks gives field sum ~ at distance 0.5: 4 * 1/0.25 = 16. Hmm that's big. At distance 1: 4/1=4. So the cavity around a 4-block cluster has radius ~1 block-ish. Block scale is (4,2) tiles per block. So cavity ~ few tiles wide per cluster, and clusters are separated (only 50% fill, and clusters need >3 cells after merges).

Wait but also num3 is the second-highest cluster field. So the cavity exists where the SUM of best+second field > 3.5. Hmm, interesting: so between two clusters, the sum of fields can exceed 3.5 too, making the cavities merge into channels. That's how you get narrow connected tunnels!

Actually wait, num7 = num + num3 where num is the strongest cluster field, num3 second strongest. If a tile is inside cluster A (field high) and near cluster B, sum > 3.5 → carve. Between two clusters the fields add up → carve a narrow connection. That's the key mechanism producing "narrow crevices" — the cavity is essentially the union of metaball isosurfaces from tiny point clusters, which yields thin worm-like channels rather than big rooms.

Now the user says: our replica has 3.9 tiles/cavity vs vanilla 1.5. So the replica's cavities are bigger/connected. We need to read DesertPass.ts and find where the field computation deviates: e.g., threshold 3.5 changed, missing the num3 second-cluster term, cluster generation fill probability, the AttemptClaim merging, the ellipse `flag` check, distance squared in block space vs tile space, etc.

1	using System;
2	using Microsoft.Xna.Framework;
3	using Terraria.WorldBuilding;
4	
5	namespace Terraria.GameContent.Biomes.Desert;
6	
7	public static class PitEntrance
8	{
9		public static void Place(DesertDescription description, GenerationProgress progress, float progressMin, float progressMax)
10		{
11			int holeRadius = WorldGen.genRand.Next(6, 9);
12			Point center = description.CombinedArea.Center;
13			center.Y = description.Surface[center.X];
14			PlaceAt(description, center, holeRadius, progress, progressMin, progressMax);
15		}
16	
17		private static void PlaceAt(DesertDescription description, Point position, int holeRadius, GenerationProgress progress, float progressMin, float progressMax)
18		{
19			int num = holeRadius + 3;
20			int num2 = num + holeRadius + 3;
21			for (int i = -holeRadius - 3; i < holeRadius + 3; i++)
22			{
23				progress.Set((float)(i + num) / (float)num2, progressMin, progressMax);
24				for (int j = description.Surface[i + position.X]; j <= description.Hive.Top + 10; j++)
25				{
26					double value = (double)(j - description.Surface[i + position.X]) / (double)(description.Hive.Top - description.Desert.Top);
27					value = Utils.Clamp(value, 0.0, 1.0);
28					int num3 = (int)(GetHoleRadiusScaleAt(value) * (double)holeRadius);
29					if (Math.Abs(i) < num3)
30					{
31						Main.tile[i + position.X, j].ClearEverything();
32					}
33					else if (Math.Abs(i) < num3 + 3 && value > 0.35)
34					{
35						Main.tile[i + position.X, j].ResetToType(397);
36					}
37					double num4 = Math.Abs((double)i / (double)holeRadius);
38					num4 *= num4;
39					if (Math.Abs(i) < num3 + 3 && (double)(j - position.Y) > 15.0 - 3.0 * num4)
40					{
41						Main.tile[i + position.X, j].wall = 187;
42						WorldGen.SquareWallFrame(i + position.X, j - 1);
43						WorldGen.SquareWallFrame(i + position.X, j);
44					}
45				}
46			}
47			holeRadius += 4;
48			for (int k = -holeRadius; k < holeRadius; k++)
49			{
50				int num5 = holeRadius - Math.Abs(k);
51				num5 = Math.Min(10, num5 * num5);
52				for (int l = 0; l < num5; l++)
53				{
54					Main.tile[k + position.X, l + description.Surface[k + position.X]].ClearEverything();
55				}
56			}
57		}
58	
59		private static double GetHoleRadiusScaleAt(double yProgress)
60		{
61			if (yProgress < 0.6)
62			{
63				return 1.0;
64			}
65			return (1.0 - SmootherStep((yProgress - 0.6) / 0.4)) * 0.5 + 0.5;
66		}
67	
68		private static double SmootherStep(double delta)
69		{
70			delta = Utils.Clamp(delta, 0.0, 1.0);
71			return 1.0 - Math.Cos(delta * 3.1415927410125732) * 0.5 - 0.5;
72		}
73	}
74

27			int num2 = int.MaxValue;
28			int num3 = 0;
29			for (int i = 0; i < heights.Length; i++)
30			{
31				num3 += heights[i];
32				num = Math.Max(num, heights[i]);
33				num2 = Math.Min(num2, heights[i]);
34			}
35			if ((double)num > Main.worldSurface - 10.0)
36			{
37				num = (int)Main.worldSurface - 10;
38			}
39			Bottom = num;
40			Top = num2;
41			Average = (double)num3 / (double)_heights.Length;
42		}
43	
44		public static SurfaceMap FromArea(int startX, int width)
45		{
46			int num = Main.maxTilesY / 2;
47			short[] array = new short[width];
48			for (int i = startX; i < startX + width; i++)
49			{
50				bool flag = false;
51				int num2 = 0;
52				for (int j = 50; j < 50 + num; j++)
53				{
54					if (Main.tile[i, j].active())
55					{
56						if (TileID.Sets.Clouds[Main.tile[i, j].type])
57						{
58							flag = false;
59						}
60						else if (!flag)
61						{
62							num2 = j;
63							flag = true;
64						}
65					}
66					if (!flag)
67					{
68						num2 = num + 50;
69					}
70				}
71				array[i - startX] = (short)num2;
72			}
73			return new SurfaceMap(array, startX);
74		}
75	}
76	

```

19	function surfaceMapFromArea(st: TileStore, startX: number, width: number) {

21	  const hs = new Int16Array(width);

31	          else if (!flag) { y = j; flag = true; }

35	    }
36	    hs[i] = y; sum += y;
37	    if (y < mn) mn = y;

69	export function desertPlaceMound(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, worldSurface: number, skipCheck = false): DesertDesc | null {
70	  _wsRef = () => worldSurface;
71	  const { w, h } = st;
72	  const factor = w / 4200;
73	  const blockCols = Math.trunc(80 * factor);
74	  const blockRows = Math.trunc((rng.next() * 0.5 + 1.5) * 170 * factor);
75	  const width = Math.trunc(4 * blockCols);   // BlockScale.X=4
76	  const height = Math.trunc(2 * blockRows);  // BlockScale.Y=2
77	  const ox = x - Math.trunc(width / 2);      // origin.X -= num4/2（int 除）
78	  const sm = surfaceMapFromArea(st, ox - 5, width + 10);
79	  if (!skipCheck && rowHasInvalidTiles(st, ox, sm.bottom, width)) return null;
80	  // num6 = (int)(Average + Bottom) / 2 —— 先截断再 int 除（DesertDescription.cs:81）
81	  const topY = Math.trunc(sm.average + sm.bottom) / 2 | 0;
82	  const hiveTop = topY + rng.int(40, 59);    // Next(40,60)
83	  const desertH = hiveTop + Math.trunc(height / 2) - topY; // Desert.Height（未封顶）
84	  // ---- SandMound.Place（SandMound.cs 1:1）----
85	  let moundH = Math.min(desertH, Math.trunc(height / 2));  // min(Desert.Height, Hive.Height/2)
86	  const moundBottom = topY + moundH;                        // capped desert.Bottom
87	  const sandBottom = topY + desertH;                        // Desert.Bottom（沙柱底）
88	  let coarse = 0, fine = 0;
89	  for (let i = -5; i < width + 5; i++) {
90	    const cx = ox + i;
91	    let t = Math.abs((i + 5) / (width + 10)) * 2 - 1;
92	    t = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, t));
93	    if (i % 3 === 0) coarse = Math.max(-10, Math.min(10, coarse + rng.int(-1, 1)));
94	    fine = Math.max(-10, Math.min(10, fine + rng.int(-1, 1)));
95	    const profile = Math.sqrt(1 - t * t * t * t);
96	    const surfY = moundBottom - Math.trunc(profile * moundH) + coarse;
97	    if (Math.abs(t) < 1 && cx >= 0 && cx < w) {
98	      // UnclampedSmoothStep=纯线性斜坡（Utils.cs:141-144），再立方——非平滑曲线
99	      const ramp = (Math.abs(t) - 0.5) / 0.3;
100	      const val = Math.min(10 + Math.trunc(topY - ramp * ramp * ramp * 20) + fine, surfY);
101	      const si = Math.max(0, Math.min(sm.heights.length - 1, i + 5)); // Surface[cx]=heights[cx-(ox-5)]
102	      const jStart = sm.heights[si] - 1;
103	      for (let j = jStart; j < val; j++) {
104	        if (j < 0 || j >= h) continue;
105	        const ti = st.idx(cx, j);
106	        st.flags[ti] = 0;          // active(false)（type 保留=幽灵，原版语义）
107	        st.wall[ti] = 0;
108	      }
109	    }
110	    // PlaceSandColumn：从 surfY 填到 sandBottom（沙/清液体/激活；height 可为负→零次）
111	    for (let y = surfY + (sandBottom - surfY) - 1; y >= surfY; y--) {
112	      if (y < 0 || y >= h) continue;
113	      const ti = st.idx(cx, y);
114	      st.type[ti] = SAND; st.flags[ti] = 1;
115	      st.liquid[ti] = 0; st.liquidType[ti] = 0;
116	    }
117	  }
118	  // UpdateSurfaceMap（mound 后重扫，入口/Hive 消费）
119	  const sm2 = surfaceMapFromArea(st, ox - 5, width + 10);

122	    hiveTop, hiveH: height, desertH,
123	    blockCols, blockRows,
124	    surface: sm.heights, surfaceX: ox - 5,

127	}

138	/** 放置循环 + Description + SandMound（Sub-A；Hive/入口在 runDesertPass 续） */
139	export function runDesertMoundOnly(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): DesertDesc {
140	  const { w } = st;
141	  const half = Math.floor(w / 2);
142	  // DesertBiome pass 放置循环（cs:12538-12562 1:1）：失败重掷**永不早退**，
143	  // 2 次换向后 skipDesertTileCheck 恒过（此前 tries>w/2 早退=可能整图无沙漠）
144	  let side = gs.dungeonSide, tries = 0, flips = 0, skipCheck = false;
145	  let num4 = Math.trunc(rng.nextIntRange(0, half) / 8) + Math.trunc(half / 8);
146	  let x = half + num4 * -side;
147	  let desc: DesertDesc | null = null;
148	  for (;;) {
149	    desc = desertPlaceMound(st, rng, x, gs.worldSurface);
150	    if (desc) break;
151	    num4 = Math.trunc(rng.nextIntRange(0, half) / 2) + Math.trunc(half / 8)
152	      + rng.nextIntRange(0, Math.trunc(tries / 12));
153	    x = half + num4 * -side;
154	    if (++tries > Math.floor(w / 4)) {
155	      side = (-side) as 1 | -1;
156	      tries = 0;
157	      if (++flips >= 2) skipCheck = true;
158	    }
159	    if (skipCheck) {
160	      // 恒过：RowHasInvalidTiles 直接 false（GenVars.skipDesertTileCheck 语义）
161	      desc = desertPlaceMound(st, rng, x, gs.worldSurface, true);

172	export function runDesertEntrancesAndHive(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, desc: DesertDesc, afterEntrance?: () => void): void {

179	  // 两侧一致省略。quitOnFail 恒 false → apply 返回 false 不中断遍历。

181	  abstract class Ga {

184	    abstract apply(ox: number, oy: number, x: number, y: number): boolean;
185	    protected ua(ox: number, oy: number, x: number, y: number): boolean {

191	    for (let i = 0; i < xs.length - 1; i++) xs[i]!.next = xs[i + 1]!;

296	  function genCircle(ox: number, oy: number, hR: number, vR: number, act: Ga): void {

299	      const n2 = hR / vR * (i - oy);

323	  function genOutline(ox: number, oy: number, d: Set<number>, act: Ga): void {  // OuterOutline

333	  function plotLine(x0: number, y0: number, x1: number, y1: number,
334	    plot: (x: number, y: number) => boolean, jump: boolean): boolean {   // Utils.PlotLine
335	    if (x0 === x1 && y0 === y1) return plot(x0, y0);
336	    const flag = Math.abs(y1 - y0) > Math.abs(x1 - x0);
337	    if (flag) { const tx = x0; x0 = y0; y0 = tx; const tx1 = x1; x1 = y1; y1 = tx1; }
338	    const num = Math.abs(x1 - x0), num2 = Math.abs(y1 - y0);
339	    let num3 = Math.trunc(num / 2), num4 = y0;
340	    const num5 = x0 < x1 ? 1 : -1, num6 = y0 < y1 ? 1 : -1;
341	    for (let i = x0; i !== x1; i += num5) {
342	      if (!plot(flag ? num4 : i, flag ? i : num4)) return false;
343	      num3 -= num2;
344	      if (num3 >= 0) continue;
345	      num4 += num6;
346	      if (!jump && !plot(flag ? num4 : i, flag ? i : num4)) return false;
347	      num3 += num;
348	    }
349	    return true;
350	  }
351	  function genTail(ox: number, oy: number, width: number, ex: number, ey: number, act: Ga): void {
352	    // Shapes.Tail：像素域（×16）画楔形尾迹（Utils.PlotTileTale）
353	    const halfWidth = width * 8;                 // (width*16)/2
354	    const sx = ox << 4, sy = oy << 4;
355	    const endX = sx + ex * 16, endY = sy + ey * 16;
356	    const vx = endX - sx, vy = endY - sy;
357	    const len = Math.sqrt(vx * vx + vy * vy);
358	    const px = -(vy / len), py = vx / len;       // 单位法向
359	    const psx = Math.trunc(sx / 16), psy = Math.trunc(sy / 16);
360	    const pex = Math.trunc(endX / 16), pey = Math.trunc(endY / 16);
361	    let length = 0;
362	    plotLine(psx, psy, pex, pey, () => { length++; return true; }, true);
363	    length--;
364	    let curLength = 0;
365	    plotLine(psx, psy, pex, pey, (x, y) => {
366	      const num = 1 - curLength / length;
367	      curLength++;
368	      const p2x = Math.trunc((sx - px * halfWidth * num) / 16), p2y = Math.trunc((sy - py * halfWidth * num) / 16);
369	      const p3x = Math.trunc((sx + px * halfWidth * num) / 16), p3y = Math.trunc((sy + py * halfWidth * num) / 16);
370	      return plotLine(x + p2x - psx, y + p2y - psy, x + p3x - psx, y + p3y - psy,
371	        (tx, ty) => { act.apply(ox, oy, tx, ty); return true; }, false);
372	    }, true);
373	  }
374	
375	  // ================= Sub-C：入口（DesertBiome.cs:23-40）=================
376	  // ChanceOfEntrance 被 Configuration.json 覆盖为 0.5（DesertBiome.cs:11 代码默认
377	  // 0.3333 不生效——曾按 0.3333 写导致门判定与原版分叉）
378	  if (rng.next() <= 0.5) {
379	    const kind = rng.nextIntRange(0, 4);        // Next(4)：Chambers/Anthill/LarvaHole/Pit
380	    if (kind === 0) chambersEntrance();
381	    else if (kind === 1) anthillEntrance();
382	    else if (kind === 2) larvaHoleEntrance();
383	    else pitEntrance();
384	  }
385	  afterEntrance?.();
386	
387	  // ---- ChambersEntrance.cs 1:1：地表沙盘 + 之字形下行隧道 ----
388	  function chambersEntrance(): void {
389	    const centerX = desc.combX + Math.trunc(desc.combW / 2);   // Desert.Center.X
390	    const px = centerX + rng.int(-40, 40);                     // Next(-40,41)
391	    const py = surf2(px);
392	    const shapeData = new Set<number>();
393	    const data = new Set<number>();
394	    const ox = px, oy = py + 2;
395	    genCircle(ox, oy, 24, 12, chain(new Blotches(), outA(new SetTile(SAND), shapeData)));
396	    const hnum = desc.hiveTop - py;                            // Hive.Top - position.Y
397	    let num2 = rng.int(0, 1) !== 0 ? 1 : -1;                   // Next(2) != 0
398	    const list: Array<[number, number, number]> = [[px + -num2 * 26, py - 8, num2]];
399	    const num3 = rng.int(2, 3);                                // Next(2,4)
400	    for (let i = 0; i < num3; i++) {
401	      const num4 = Math.trunc((i + 1) / num3 * hnum) + rng.int(-8, 8);
402	      const num5 = num2 * rng.int(20, 40);
403	      const num6 = rng.int(18, 28);
404	      genCircle(px, py, Math.trunc(num6 / 2), 3, chain(
405	        new Offset(num5, num4), new Blotches(), outA(new Clear(), data), new PlaceWall(187)));
406	      list.push([num5 + Math.trunc(num6 / 2) * -num2 + px, num4 + py, -num2]);
407	      num2 *= -1;
408	    }
409	    genOutline(px, py, data, chain(new Expand(1), new OnlyTiles(SAND), new SetTile(HARDSAND), new PlaceWall(187)));
410	    const pairAct = chain(new IsSolid(), new Blotches(), new Clear(), new Expand(1),
411	      new PlaceWall(187), new OnlyTiles(SAND), new SetTile(HARDSAND));
412	    for (let j = 1; j < list.length; j++) {
413	      const pa = list[j - 1]!, pb = list[j]!;
414	      const connLen = Math.abs(pb[0] - pa[0]) * 1.5;
415	      for (let t = 0; t <= 1; t += 0.02) {
416	        const ax = pa[0] + pa[2] * connLen * t, ay = pa[1];
417	        const bx = pb[0] + pb[2] * connLen * (1 - t), by = pb[1];
418	        const cx = pa[0] + (pb[0] - pa[0]) * t, cy = pa[1] + (pb[1] - pa[1]) * t;   // Lerp(p0,p1,t)
419	        const dx = ax + (cx - ax) * t, dy = ay + (cy - ay) * t;                      // Lerp(v,v3,t)
420	        const ex = cx + (bx - cx) * t, ey = cy + (by - cy) * t;                      // Lerp(v3,v2,t)
421	        const fx = dx + (ex - dx) * t, fy = dy + (ey - dy) * t;                      // Lerp(v4,v5,t)
422	        genRectWH(Math.trunc(fx), Math.trunc(fy), 2, 4, pairAct);                    // .ToPoint()
423	      }
424	    }
425	    genRect(ox, oy, -29, -12, 29, 0, chain(new NotInShape(shapeData), new Expand(1), new PlaceWall(0)));
426	  }
427	
428	  // ---- AnthillEntrance.cs 1:1：蚁丘沙锥 + 螺旋下行竖井 ----
429	  function anthillEntrance(): void {
430	    const num = rng.int(2, 3);                                  // Next(2,4)
431	    for (let i = 0; i < num; i++) {
432	      const holeRadius = rng.int(15, 17);                       // Next(15,18)
433	      const num2 = Math.trunc((i + 1) / (num + 1) * (desc.combW + 10)) + desc.combX;  // Surface.Width
434	      anthillAt(num2, surf2(num2), holeRadius);
435	    }
436	  }
437	  function anthillAt(px: number, py: number, holeRadius: number): void {
438	    const data = new Set<number>();
439	    const ox = px, oy = py + 6;
440	    genTail(ox, oy, holeRadius * 2, 0, -holeRadius * 1.5, outA(new SetTile(SAND), data));
441	    const pairA = chain(new Blotches(), new IsSolid(), new Clear(), new PlaceWall(187));
442	    const pairB = chain(new IsSolid(), new Clear(), new PlaceWall(187));
443	    const pairC = chain(new IsSolid(), new SetTile(HARDSAND), new PlaceWall(187));
444	    const pairD = chain(new SkipWalls(187), new SetTile(SAND));
445	    const pairE = new PlaceWall(187);
446	    let num = px;
447	    for (let i = py - holeRadius - 3; i < desc.hiveTop + (py - desc.combY) * 2 + 12; i++) {
448	      genRectWH(num, i, 1, 1, i < py ? pairB : pairA);
449	      genCircle(num, i, 2, 3, pairC);
450	      if (i % 3 === 0 && i >= py) {
451	        num += rng.int(-1, 1);                                  // Next(-1,2)
452	        genRectWH(num, i, 1, 1, pairA);
453	        if (i >= py + 5) {
454	          genCircle(num, i, holeRadius, 3, pairD);
455	          genCircle(num, i, holeRadius - 2, 3, pairE);
456	        }
457	        genCircle(num, i, 2, 3, pairC);
458	      }
459	    }
460	    genCircle(ox, oy - Math.trunc(holeRadius * 1.5) + 3, Math.trunc(holeRadius / 2), Math.trunc(holeRadius / 3),
461	      chain(new ClearTile(), new Expand(1), new PlaceWall(0)));
462	    // All(data)+Smooth：仅坡面/半砖——哈希无关，两侧一致省略
463	  }
464	
465	  // ---- LarvaHoleEntrance.cs 1:1：地表空腔 + 尾迹洞 + 蜿蜒竖井 ----
466	  function larvaHoleEntrance(): void {
467	    const num = rng.int(2, 3);
468	    for (let i = 0; i < num; i++) {
469	      const holeRadius = rng.int(13, 15);                       // Next(13,16)
470	      const num2 = Math.trunc((i + 1) / (num + 1) * (desc.combW + 10)) + desc.combX;
471	      larvaAt(num2, surf2(num2), holeRadius);
472	    }
473	  }
474	  function larvaAt(px: number, py: number, holeRadius: number): void {
475	    const data = new Set<number>();
476	    genRect(px, py, -holeRadius, -holeRadius * 2, holeRadius, 0, outA(new Clear(), data));
477	    genTail(px, py, holeRadius * 2, 0, holeRadius * 1.5, outA(new Clear(), data));
478	    // All(data)+Offset(0,1)+Expand(1)+IsSolid+Smooth —— 仅坡面，哈希无关省略
479	    const pairA = chain(new Blotches(), new IsSolid(), new Clear(), new PlaceWall(187));
480	    const pairB = chain(new IsSolid(), new SetTile(HARDSAND), new PlaceWall(187));
481	    let num = px;
482	    for (let i = py + Math.trunc(holeRadius * 1.5); i < desc.hiveTop + (py - desc.combY) * 2 + 12; i++) {
483	      genRectWH(num, i, 1, 1, pairA);
484	      genCircle(num, i, 2, 3, pairB);
485	      if (i % 3 === 0) {
486	        num += rng.int(-1, 1);
487	        genRectWH(num, i, 1, 1, pairA);
488	        genCircle(num, i, 2, 3, pairB);
489	      }
490	    }
491	    genAll(px, py + 2, data, new PlaceWall(0));
492	  }
493	
494	  // ---- PitEntrance.cs 1:1（零掷骰）：漏斗形直落竖井 ----
495	  function pitEntrance(): void {
496	    const holeRadius = rng.int(6, 8);                           // Next(6,9)
497	    const px = desc.combX + Math.trunc(desc.combW / 2), py = surf2(desc.combX + Math.trunc(desc.combW / 2));
498	    const smootherStep = (delta: number) => {
499	      const d = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, delta));
500	      return 1 - Math.cos(d * 3.1415927410125732) * 0.5 - 0.5;   // C# (float)π 常量
501	    };
502	    const scaleAt = (yProgress: number) => yProgress < 0.6 ? 1
503	      : (1 - smootherStep((yProgress - 0.6) / 0.4)) * 0.5 + 0.5;
504	    for (let i = -holeRadius - 3; i < holeRadius + 3; i++) {
505	      const cx = i + px;
506	      if (cx < 0 || cx >= W) continue;
507	      const sj = surf2(cx);
508	      for (let j = sj; j <= desc.hiveTop + 10; j++) {
509	        if (j < 0 || j >= H) continue;
510	        let value = (j - sj) / (desc.hiveTop - desc.combY);
511	        value = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, value));
512	        const n3 = Math.trunc(scaleAt(value) * holeRadius);
513	        if (Math.abs(i) < n3) clearEverything(cx, j);
514	        else if (Math.abs(i) < n3 + 3 && value > 0.35) resetToType(cx, j, HARDSAND);
515	        let n4 = Math.abs(i / holeRadius);
516	        n4 *= n4;
517	        if (Math.abs(i) < n3 + 3 && (j - py) > 15 - 3 * n4) st.wall[st.idx(cx, j)] = 187;
518	      }
519	    }
520	    const hr2 = holeRadius + 4;
521	    for (let k = -hr2; k < hr2; k++) {
522	      const cx = k + px;
523	      if (cx < 0 || cx >= W) continue;
524	      let n5 = hr2 - Math.abs(k);
525	      n5 = Math.min(10, n5 * n5);
526	      for (let l = 0; l < n5; l++) {
527	        const y = l + surf2(cx);
528	        if (y < 0 || y >= H) continue;
529	        clearEverything(cx, y);
530	      }
531	    }
532	  }
533	
534	  // ================= Sub-B：DesertHive（DesertHive.cs 1:1）=================
535	  // ---- ClusterGroup.Generate（椭圆散点→深度2 DFS→AttemptClaim→≥4 重组）----
536	  const gw = desc.blockCols, gh = desc.blockRows;
537	  const blockMap: boolean[][] = [];
538	  for (let c = 0; c < gw; c++) blockMap.push(new Array(gh).fill(false));
539	  {
540	    const num1 = Math.trunc(gw / 2) - 1, num2 = Math.trunc(gh / 2) - 1;
541	    const num3 = (num1 + 1) * (num1 + 1);
542	    for (let r = 0; r <= num2 * 2; r++) {       // 原版 i ∈ [0, 2*(H/2-1)]——末行不散点！
543	      const num4 = num1 / num2 * (r - num2);
544	      const num5 = Math.min(num1, Math.trunc(Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, num3 - num4 * num4))));
545	      for (let c = num1 - num5; c <= num1 + num5; c++) blockMap[c]![r] = rng.int(0, 1) === 0;
546	    }
547	  }
548	  const pointClusters: Array<Array<[number, number]>> = [];
549	  {
550	    const dfs = (x: number, y: number, level: number, acc: Array<[number, number]>) => {
551	      acc.push([x, y]);
552	      blockMap[x]![y] = false;
553	      const lv = level - 1;
554	      if (lv === -1) return;
555	      if (x > 0 && blockMap[x - 1]![y]) dfs(x - 1, y, lv, acc);
556	      if (x < gw - 1 && blockMap[x + 1]![y]) dfs(x + 1, y, lv, acc);
557	      if (y > 0 && blockMap[x]![y - 1]) dfs(x, y - 1, lv, acc);
558	      if (y < gh - 1 && blockMap[x]![y + 1]) dfs(x, y + 1, lv, acc);
559	    };
560	    for (let x = 0; x < gw; x++) {
561	      for (let y = 0; y < gh; y++) {
562	        if (blockMap[x]![y] && rng.int(0, 1) === 0) {
563	          const pc: Array<[number, number]> = [];
564	          dfs(x, y, 2, pc);
565	          if (pc.length > 2) pointClusters.push(pc);
566	        }
567	      }
568	    }
569	  }
570	  const idxMap: number[][] = [];
571	  for (let c = 0; c < gw; c++) idxMap.push(new Array(gh).fill(-1));
572	  pointClusters.forEach((pc, i) => { for (const [qx, qy] of pc) idxMap[qx]![qy] = i; });
573	  for (let ci = 0; ci < pointClusters.length; ci++) {
574	    for (const [px, py] of pointClusters[ci]!) {
575	      if (idxMap[px]![py] === -1) break;        // 原版 foreach 内 break
576	      const idx = idxMap[px]![py];
577	      const claim = (nx: number, ny: number) => {
578	        if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= gw || ny >= gh) return;   // 原版由调用点边界门保证
579	        const other = idxMap[nx]![ny];
580	        if (other === -1 || other === idx) return;
581	        const val = rng.int(0, 1) === 0 ? -1 : idx;
582	        for (const [qx, qy] of pointClusters[other]!) idxMap[qx]![qy] = val;
583	      };
584	      claim(px - 1, py); claim(px + 1, py); claim(px, py - 1); claim(px, py + 1);
585	    }
586	  }
587	  for (const pc of pointClusters) pc.length = 0;
588	  for (let x = 0; x < gw; x++) {
589	    for (let y = 0; y < gh; y++) {
590	      if (idxMap[x]![y] !== -1) pointClusters[idxMap[x]![y]]!.push([x, y]);
591	    }
592	  }
593	  for (const pc of pointClusters) if (pc.length < 4) pc.length = 0;
594	  const finalClusters: Array<Array<[number, number]>> = [];
595	  for (const pc of pointClusters) {
596	    if (pc.length <= 0) continue;
597	    finalClusters.push(pc.map(([bx, by]) => [
598	      bx + (rng.next() - 0.5) * 0.5, by + (rng.next() - 0.5) * 0.5,
599	    ] as [number, number]));
600	  }
601	
602	  // ---- PlaceClustersArea（场强分层）----
603	  // ★shell 类型骰与 0.25 门走 FastRandom（DesertHive.cs:253/366）——与 genRand
604	  //  完全独立的第二条流，曾误用 rng.int/rng.next 造成每格 1-2 次幽灵掷骰
605	  const hiveW = desc.combW, hiveH = desc.hiveH;
606	  const fr = new FastRandom(gs.seed).withModifier(57005);
607	  for (let hi = -20; hi < hiveW + 20; hi++) {
608	    for (let hj = -20; hj < hiveH + 20; hj++) {
609	      const px = desc.combX + hi, py = desc.hiveTop + hj;
610	      if (px < 1 || px >= W - 1 || py < 1 || py >= H - 1) continue;   // InWorld(i,j,1)
611	      let shellType: number = SAND;
612	      if (fr.nextMax(3) === 0) shellType = HARDSAND;                  // fastRandom.Next(3)
613	      const vx = (hi - 2) / hiveW * gw;       // (p-BlockScale/2)/Hive*Cluster
614	      const vy = (hj - 1) / hiveH * gh;
615	      let f1 = 0, f3 = 0, f2 = -1;
616	      for (let k = 0; k < finalClusters.length; k++) {
617	        const cl = finalClusters[k]!;
618	        const c0 = cl[0]!;
619	        if (Math.abs(c0[0] - vx) > 10 || Math.abs(c0[1] - vy) > 10) continue;
620	        let f6 = 0;
621	        for (const [bx, by] of cl) {
622	          const ddx = bx - vx, ddy = by - vy;
623	          f6 += 1 / (ddx * ddx + ddy * ddy);
624	        }
625	        if (f6 > f1) { if (f1 > f3) f3 = f1; f1 = f6; f2 = k; }
626	        else if (f6 > f3) f3 = f6;
627	      }
628	      const field = f1 + f3;
629	      const ex = (hi - 2) / hiveW * 2 - 1;    // (p-BlockScale/2)/Hive*2-One
630	      const ey = (hj - 1) / hiveH * 2 - 1;
631	      const isEdge = Math.sqrt(ex * ex + ey * ey) >= 0.8;   // Vector2D.Length()
632	      const i = st.idx(px, py);
633	      if (field > 3.5) {
634	        clearEverything(px, py);
635	        st.wall[i] = 187;
636	        if (f2 % 15 === 2) resetToType(px, py, FOSSIL);     // 获胜簇索引 %15==2
637	      } else if (field > 1.8) {
638	        st.wall[i] = 187;
639	        if (py < gs.worldSurface) st.liquid[i] = 0;         // else lava()：液量不变
640	        else st.liquidType[i] = 2;                          // 仅置岩浆类型位（原版事实）
641	        if (!isEdge || st.flags[i]) resetToType(px, py, SANDSTONE);
642	      } else if (field > 0.7 || !isEdge) {
643	        st.wall[i] = 216;
644	        st.liquid[i] = 0;
645	        if (!isEdge || st.flags[i]) resetToType(px, py, shellType);
646	      } else if (field > 0.25) {
647	        const fr2 = fr.withModifierXY(hi, hj);              // fastRandom.WithModifier(x,y)
648	        if (fr2.nextDouble() < (field - 0.25) / 0.45) {
649	          st.wall[i] = 187;
650	          if (py < gs.worldSurface) st.liquid[i] = 0;
651	          else st.liquidType[i] = 2;
652	          if (st.flags[i]) resetToType(px, py, shellType);
653	        }
654	      }
655	    }
656	  }
657	
658	  // ---- AddTileVariance（DesertHive.cs:413-499）----
659	  // 第一遍：沙 53（含幽灵 53，不判 active）下方 2 格有非实心 → 酥化 397
660	  for (let vi = -20; vi < hiveW + 20; vi++) {
661	    for (let vj = -20; vj < hiveH + 20; vj++) {
662	      const px = desc.combX + vi, py = desc.hiveTop + vj;
663	      if (px < 1 || px >= W - 1 || py < 1 || py >= H - 1) continue;
664	      const s1 = py + 1 < H && solidAt(px, py + 1);
665	      const s2 = py + 2 < H && solidAt(px, py + 2);
666	      const i = st.idx(px, py);
667	      if (st.type[i] === SAND && (!s1 || !s2)) st.type[i] = HARDSAND;
668	    }
669	  }
670	  // 第二遍：396 顶部装饰（485/751/484/165/187）——放置两侧一致省略（PlaceTile
671	  // 是巨型框架，装饰为 1x1 非实心物），掷骰照原序消耗保持流对齐
672	  for (let wk = -20; wk < hiveW + 20; wk++) {
673	    for (let wl = -20; wl < hiveH + 20; wl++) {
674	      const px = desc.combX + wk, py = desc.hiveTop + wl;
675	      if (px < 5 || px >= W - 5 || py < 5 || py >= H - 5) continue;   // InWorld(,5)
676	      const i = st.idx(px, py);
677	      if (!st.flags[i] || st.type[i] !== SANDSTONE) continue;
678	      let flag = true;
679	      for (let n = -1; n >= -3; n--) {
680	        const y = py + n;
681	        if (y >= 0 && ((inW(px, y) && st.flags[st.idx(px, y)]) || (inW(px + 1, y) && st.flags[st.idx(px + 1, y)]))) { flag = false; break; }
682	      }
683	      let flag2 = true;
684	      for (let m = 1; m <= 3; m++) {
685	        const y = py + m;
686	        if (y < H && ((inW(px, y) && st.flags[st.idx(px, y)]) || (inW(px + 1, y) && st.flags[st.idx(px + 1, y)]))) { flag2 = false; break; }
687	      }
688	      if (flag && rng.int(0, 19) === 0) {      // Next(20)==0 → 485/751
689	        rng.int(0, 3);                         // style = Next(4)
690	        rng.int(0, 29);                        // Next(30)==0 → 751
691	      } else if (flag && rng.int(0, 4) === 0) {          // 484
692	      } else if ((flag !== flag2) && rng.int(0, 4) === 0) {  // 165
693	      } else if (flag && rng.int(0, 4) === 0) {          // 187
694	      }
695	    }
696	  }

698	  // ExportDescriptionToEngine（DesertBiome.cs:54-58）：CombinedArea.Inflate(10,10)。

123	//   rng.range(a,b) = a + NextDouble()*(b-a)
124	export class RNG {
125	  private ur: UnifiedRandom;
126	  constructor(seed: number) {
127	    this.ur = new UnifiedRandom(seed);
128	  }
129	
130	  next(): number {
131	    return this.ur.nextDouble();
132	  }
133	
134	  /** 闭区间 [min, max]，≡ 原版 Next(min, max+1) */
135	  int(min: number, max: number): number {
136	    return this.ur.nextIntRange(min, max + 1);
137	  }
138	
139	  range(min: number, max: number): number {
140	    return min + this.ur.nextDouble() * (max - min);
141	  }
142	
143	  chance(p: number): boolean {
144	    return this.ur.nextDouble() < p;
145	  }
146	
147	  pick<T>(arr: T[]): T {
148	    return arr[this.ur.nextInt(arr.length)];
149	  }
150	
151	  /** 原版裸方法直通（Reset 序列/pass 级对账用，语义与 C# 完全一致） */
152	  nextInt(maxValue: number): number {
153	    return this.ur.nextInt(maxValue);
154	  }
155	
156	  nextIntRange(minValue: number, maxValue: number): number {
157	    return this.ur.nextIntRange(minValue, maxValue);
158	  }
159	
160	  get vanilla(): UnifiedRandom {
161	    return this.ur;
162	  }
163	}
164	
165	// ── Crc32（ReLogic.Utilities.Crc32 反编译 1:1）─────────────────────────────
166	// 标准 IEEE 表（多项式 0xEDB88320）。按 UTF-16 code unit 逐字符：
167	// (num ^ c) & 0xFF 只取 charCode 低字节——charCode > 0xFF 时高字节不参与。
168	const CRC32_TAB = (() => {
169	  const t = new Uint32Array(256);
170	  for (let i = 0; i < 256; i++) {
171	    let c = i;
172	    for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) c = c & 1 ? 0xedb88320 ^ (c >>> 1) : c >>> 1;
173	    t[i] = c >>> 0;
174	  }
175	  return t;
176	})();
177	
178	export function crc32Calculate(value: string): number {
179	  let num = 0xffffffff;
180	  for (let i = 0; i < value.length; i++) {
181	    num = (CRC32_TAB[(num ^ value.charCodeAt(i)) & 0xff] ^ (num >>> 8)) >>> 0;
182	  }
183	  return ~num | 0; // C# (int)(~uint)：位翻转后按 int32 解释（可为负）
184	}
185	
186	// ── 种子解析（WorldFileData.TranslateSeed, cs:399-410 1:1）─────────────────
187	// 数字字符串 → Abs(int)（int.MinValue 特判）；否则 Crc32 直出。
188	// C# int.TryParse 细节：前后空白/正负号/前导零合法，溢出或空串 → false 走 Crc32。
189	// （Unicode 数字字符 C# 能解析而本实现不认——种子 UI 只产 ASCII，不构成偏差。）
190	export function translateSeed(seedText: string): number {
191	  const m = /^\s*([+-]?\d+)\s*$/.exec(seedText);
192	  if (m) {
193	    const v = BigInt(m[1]);
194	    if (v >= -2147483648n && v <= 2147483647n) {
195	      const n = Number(v);
196	      return n === -2147483648 ? 2147483647 : Math.abs(n);
197	    }
198	  }
199	  return crc32Calculate(seedText);
200	}
201	
202	// FNV-1a 字符串 hash（旧种子派生/通用标识用；世界生成种子已改 translateSeed）
203	export function hashString(str: string): number {
204	  let h = 2166136261 >>> 0;
205	  for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
206	    h ^= str.charCodeAt(i);
207	    h = Math.imul(h, 16777619);
208	  }
209	  return h >>> 0;
210	}
211	
212	// 2D 整数 hash → [0,1)，无状态确定性噪声（渲染层视觉变体用，与种子等价无关）
213	export function hash2(x: number, y: number, seed: number): number {
214	  let h = seed >>> 0;
215	  h = Math.imul(h ^ (x | 0), 0x27d4eb2d);
216	  h = Math.imul(h ^ (y | 0), 0x85ebca6b);
217	  h ^= h >>> 15;
218	  h = Math.imul(h, 0x2c1b3c6d);
219	  h ^= h >>> 12;
220	  return (h >>> 0) / 4294967296;
221	}
222	
223	// ── FastRandom（Terraria.Utilities/FastRandom.cs:7-88 一比一）─────────────────
224	// 48 位 LCG（Java Random 同族），与 UnifiedRandom 完全独立的第二条随机流。
225	// 原版世界生成里凡显式 `new FastRandom(seed).WithModifier(...)` 的掷骰走这里
226	// （DesertHive.PlaceClustersArea 的 shell 类型骰/0.25 门、光照等）——绝不可用
227	// RNG/UnifiedRandom 代替，否则流偏移不可查。光照系统的副本在
228	// src/lighting/FastRandom.ts（同源、API 子集），此处是世界生成权威实现。
229	const FR_MULTIPLIER = 25214903917n;
230	const FR_ADD = 11n;
231	const FR_MASK = 0xffffffffffffn; // 2^48 - 1
232	
233	export class FastRandom {
234	  private seed: bigint;
235	
236	  constructor(seed: bigint | number) {
237	    this.seed = (typeof seed === 'bigint' ? seed : BigInt(seed)) & FR_MASK;
238	  }
239	
240	  /** NextSeed(L85-88)：静态推进，不动自身状态 */
241	  static nextSeed(seed: bigint): bigint {
242	    return (seed * FR_MULTIPLIER + FR_ADD) & FR_MASK;
243	  }
244	
245	  /** WithModifier(ulong)(L27-30)：新实例种子 = NextSeed(m) ^ 原 Seed（不改自身） */
246	  withModifier(m: number | bigint): FastRandom {
247	    const mod = typeof m === 'bigint' ? m : BigInt(m);
248	    return new FastRandom(FastRandom.nextSeed(mod) ^ this.seed);
249	  }
250	
251	  /** WithModifier(int x, int y)(L32-35)：
252	   *  modifier = (ulong)(x + 2654435769u + ((long)y << 6)) + ((ulong)y >> 2)
253	   *  坑：C# 的 (ulong)y 是 64 位补码（负 y → 巨值再右移），不能写 JS 的
254	   *  `y >>> 0`（32 位截断）——DesertHive 的 hj=-20 行曾因此整行门骰错位 */
255	  withModifierXY(x: number, y: number): FastRandom {
256	    const u64y = BigInt(y) & 0xffffffffffffffffn;   // (ulong)y：64 位补码
257	    const mod = BigInt(x + 2654435769 + (y << 6)) + (u64y >> 2n);
258	    return this.withModifier(mod);
259	  }
260	
261	  /** NextBits(b)(L47-51)：前进一步并取高 b 位 */
262	  private nextBits(bits: number): number {
263	    this.seed = FastRandom.nextSeed(this.seed);
264	    return Number(this.seed >> BigInt(48 - bits));
265	  }
266	
267	  /** Next(max)(L63-78)（= Next(min,max) 的 range 部分）；2 的幂走快速路径，
268	   *  其余拒绝采样——溢出带判据 num <= 2^31-1-(max-1) 与原版 int32 回绕等价 */
269	  nextMax(max: number): number {
270	    if ((max & -max) === max) {
271	      // (int)((long)max * NextBits(31) >> 31)
272	      return Math.floor((max * this.nextBits(31)) / 2147483648);
273	    }
274	    for (;;) {
275	      const num = this.nextBits(31);          // [0, 2^31)
276	      const rem = num % max;
277	      if (num <= 2147483647 - (max - 1)) return rem;
278	    }
279	  }
280	
281	  /** NextDouble()(L58-61)：`(float)NextBits(32) * 4.656613E-10f`——两个坑：
282	   *  1. NextBits(32)=(int)(seed>>16) 是 unchecked ulong→int 回绕，≥2^31 取负；
283	   *  2. 乘法在 float（24 位尾数）精度——必须 fround，否则与 C# 位级不等。
284	   *  结果因此可落在 [-1,1)（原版事实，DesertHive 0.25 门直接消费） */
285	  nextDouble(): number {
286	    this.seed = FastRandom.nextSeed(this.seed);
287	    const raw = Number(this.seed >> 16n);
288	    const b32 = raw >= 2 ** 31 ? raw - 2 ** 32 : raw; // (int) unchecked 回绕
289	    return Math.fround(Math.fround(b32) * Math.fround(4.656613e-10));
290	  }
291	}
292

295	        if (rockFamily(above) && !st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] && !st.flags[st.idx(x, y + 1)]) {

		public override bool Apply(Point origin, int x, int y, params object[] args)
		{
			GenBase._random.NextDouble();
			if (GenBase._random.NextDouble() < _chance)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				int num = GenBase._random.Next(1 - _minX, 1);
				int num2 = GenBase._random.Next(0, _maxX);
				int num3 = GenBase._random.Next(1 - _minY, 1);
				int num4 = GenBase._random.Next(0, _maxY);
				for (int i = num; i <= num2; i++)
				{
					for (int j = num3; j <= num4; j++)
					{
						flag |= !UnitApply(origin, x + i, y + j, args);
					}
				}
				return !flag;
			}
			return UnitApply(origin, x, y, args);
		}
	}

40	}
41	
42	function hashGrids(st: { type: Uint16Array; flags: Uint8Array; liquid: Uint8Array; wall: Uint16Array; w: number; h: number }) {
43	  const n = st.w * st.h;
44	  const mapped = new Uint16Array(n);
45	  const ghost = new Uint16Array(n);
46	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
47	    const v = V_ID[st.type[i]];
48	    if (st.flags[i]) mapped[i] = v < 0 ? 0x7fff : v;
49	    else ghost[i] = v < 0 ? 0x7fff : v; // 幽灵 parity：inactive 格的残留 type
50	  }
51	  return `${fnv1a(mapped)} ${fnv1a(st.flags)} ${fnv1a(st.liquid)}`;
52	}

57	function hashWalls(st: { wall: Uint16Array }): string {
58	  return fnv1a(st.wall);
59	}
60	
61	function hashGhost(st: { type: Uint16Array; flags: Uint8Array; w: number; h: number }): string {
62	  const n = st.w * st.h;

64	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {

67	    ghost[i] = v < 0 ? 0x7fff : v;
68	  }
69	  return fnv1a(ghost);   // fnv1a 返回 hex string（注解曾是 number——陈旧签名残留）
70	}

81	  runSandPatchesPass(st, rng, gs); out.set('sandpatches', hashGrids(st));
82	  runTunnelsPass(st, rng, gs); out.set('tunnels', hashGrids(st));
83	  runMountCavesPass(st, rng, gs); out.set('mountaincaves', hashGrids(st));
84	  runDirtWallBgPass(st, rng, gs); out.set('dirtwallbg', hashGrids(st));
85	  runRocksAndClayPass(st, rng, gs); out.set('rocksclay', hashGrids(st));
86	  runSmallHolesPass(st, rng, gs); out.set('smallholes', hashGrids(st));
87	  runCavesPass(st, rng, gs); out.set('surfacecaves', hashGrids(st));
88	  // 1456 注册序（执行序=注册序，WorldGenerator.cs:447）：
89	  //   14 SurfaceCaves → 16 IceBiome → 17 Grass → Jungle(12497) → 18 MudCaves →
90	  //   19 Desert → … → 32 Dungeon → 33 MCOpenings → 34 Beaches → 35 Gems。
91	  //   mcopenings/beaches 原紧跟 surfacecaves（注册序修正移到 Dungeon 后）。
92	  runIceBiomePass(st, rng, gs); out.set('icebiome', hashGrids(st));
93	  runGrassPass(st, rng, gs); out.set('grass', hashGrids(st));
94	  runJunglePass(st, rng, gs); out.set('jungle', hashGrids(st));
95	  runMudCavesPass(st, TILE_BY_KEY['mud']!, TILE_BY_KEY['v_60_jungle_grass_block']!);
96	  out.set('mudcaves', hashGrids(st));
97	  const desc = runDesertMoundOnly(st, rng, gs); out.set('desertmound', hashGrids(st));
98	  // Sub-C 入口 + Sub-B 蜂巢（DesertBiome.Place 顺序：入口先于 Hive）。

# 結論(TL;DR)

**`DesertPass.ts` 的蜂巢雕刻參數與原版 1:1 一致,沒有找到任何"挖得比原版大"的偏差。** 真正的偏差在下游 `GemPasses.ts` 的琥珀循環:**漏掉了原版"採樣錨點必須是空氣格 + 無岩漿"兩個前置檢查**(`WorldGen.cs:20898-20902`),導致琥珀觸發率放大約一個數量級。你測到的 "3.9 格/簇 vs 1.5 格/簇" 用這一個 bug 就能定量解釋,**不需要假設腔體更開闊**。

# 1. 原版腔體是怎麼挖出來的

關鍵認知:**原版地下沙漠蜂巢完全不是隨機走挖(沒有 TileRunner),而是"粗網格點簇 + metaball 場強分層"**。這就是為什麼腔體天生是窄縫——場強閾值要求"貼近簇",而簇又小又稀疏。

## 1.1 尺寸框架 — `DesertDescription.cs`

- `:14` `BlockScale = (4, 2)` —— 每個"塊" = 水平 4 格 × 垂直 2 格。
- `:51-53` `blockCols = 80 * (maxTilesX/4200)`(4200 寬世界 = 80 列);`blockRows = (rand*0.5+1.5) * 170 * factor`。蜂巢 tile 尺寸 = 4×blockCols 寬,2×blockRows 高。
- `:75-77` Hive 矩形 = 沙漠下半部;Desert 矩形只蓋住 Hive 上半(沙丘填沙只到 Hive 頂部一半,見 §1.4)。

## 1.2 簇生成 — `DesertHive.cs:79-190` (`ClusterGroup.Generate`)

1. **橢圓內 50% 均勻散點**(`:87-95`):`array[j,i] = genRand.Next(2)==0`,橢圓半徑 = `W/2-1`、`H/2-1`。注意循環上界 `i <= point.Y + num2`,即**最後一行不散點**。
2. **深度限制 DFS 聚簇**(`:39-63`,`level=2`):從種子點最多再走 2 步 → 每簇最多 ~13 格,**簇天生細小**。種子本身還要再過一次 `Next(2)==0`(`:101`),即只有 ~25% 的散點會成簇。簇內格數 `>2` 才保留(`:105`)。
3. **AttemptClaim 鄰簇合併/抹除**(`:65-77`, `:120-155`):相鄰簇 50% 概率被吞並或被置 -1,之後 **<4 格的簇全部丟棄**(`:170-176`)。這兩步是腔體"稀疏化"的關鍵。
4. **每格抖動 ±0.25**(`:186`),讓場強邊界不規則。

## 1.3 腔體雕刻 — `DesertHive.cs:259-411` (`PlaceClustersArea`) ★核心★

對 `Hive.Inflate(20,20)` 內每格:

- `:277-279` tile → 塊坐標:`((hi-2, hj-1) / Hive尺寸) * 簇網格尺寸`,即 1 塊 = 4×2 tile。
- `:283` 粗剔除:離簇首格 >10 塊直接跳過。
- `:290` 場強:`f += 1/dist²` 對簇內每塊求和。
- `:306` **`field = 最強簇場強 + 次強簇場強`**。次強項被加進來是點睛之筆:**相鄰簇之間的間隙因兩簇場強疊加而超過閾值 → 挖出窄通道**;簇本身周圍挖出小洞。因為簇只有 4-13 格、1/d² 衰減極快,`>3.5` 的區域只存在於"距簇 ~1 塊"之內 → 腔體天然是蠕蟲狀窄縫網,不是房間。

閾值級聯(`:312-395`),牆塗布範圍全在這裡:

| 場強 | 行為 | 牆 |
|---|---|---|
| `>3.5` (`:312`) | **腔體**:`tile.ClearEverything()`;`num2%15==2`(`:319`)→ `ResetToType(404)` 沙漠化石(腔內僅有的"柱狀物",單格化石) | **187** |
| `>1.8` (`:325`) | 砂岩(396)殼——腔體外的實心砂岩柱就是它 | **187** |
| `>0.7` 或 橢圓邊緣(`:348`) | 沙/硬化沙(33% 走 FastRandom 出 397) | **216** |
| `>0.25` (`:364`) | 每格 FastRandom 概率門 `(field-0.25)/0.45` | 187 |
| `≤0.25` 且在邊緣環 | **完全不動**——保留沙丘/洞穴期舊格 | 原樣 |

兩個隱含結構要點:
- **`flag`(橢圓歸一化長度 ≥0.8 的外環,`:308-309`)內,`:342`/`:358`/`:385` 只在 `!flag || tile.active()` 時才重寫 tile** → 外環裡**已存在的洞穴空氣會被保留**,並被 2/3 檔刷上 187/216 牆。琥珀錨定格裡有一部份根本不是蜂巢挖的,而是更早洞穴 pass 留下的空氣。
- **沒有顯式"留柱子"邏輯**——柱子就是場強 ≤3.5 的殘餘砂岩;腔內裝飾只有 404 化石。

## 1.4 沙丘預填 — `SandMound.cs:49`

`PlaceSandColumn` 從 `sqrt(1-t⁴)` 剖面填純沙(53)到 `Desert.Bottom` = `hiveTop + height/2`,即**只填滿 Hive 上半**;Hive 下半在蜂巢前是洞穴期地形。

## 1.5 入口(只碰蜂巢頂部,不影響內部開闊度)

- `ChambersEntrance.cs:64`:2-3 個 `Circle(num6/2=9..14, 3)` 橢圓室(18-29 寬 × 7 高)+ `:69` `Rectangle(2,4)` 貝塞爾隧道(注意 `Shapes.cs:155-158`:`Rectangle(w,h)` 是 **(0,0,w,h) 非居中**)。
- `AnthillEntrance.cs:32-33`:`Circle(holeRadius=15..17, 3)` 大橢圓是 **SkipWalls+SetTile(53) 填沙**,不是挖空;真正挖空的是每行 1×1 + 每 3 行的窄井。
- `PitEntrance.cs` 漏斗直井,零擲骰。
- 入口概率:**`Configuration.json:19-22` DesertBiome 塊 `ChanceOfEntrance: 0.5`**(代碼默認 0.3333 在 `DesertBiome.cs:11` 不生效)——復刻版寫 0.5 是**對的**。

# 3. 偏差清單(按影響大小排序)

## ★ 頭號偏差:`GemPasses.ts` 琥珀循環缺少錨點空氣/岩漿檢查(直接製造了你的度量)

原版 `WorldGen.cs:20895-20902`:
```csharp
int num5 = genRand.Next(20, Main.maxTilesX - 20);
int num6 = genRand.Next((int)Main.worldSurface, Main.maxTilesY - 300);
if (!Main.tile[num5, num6].active() && !Main.tile[num5, num6].anyLava()
    && (Main.tile[num5, num6].wall == 216 || Main.tile[num5, num6].wall == 187))
{   // 才擲 4 個窗口骰
```

復刻版 `GemPasses.ts:251-258`:
```ts
const wl = st.wall[st.idx(x, y)];
if (wl !== 187 && wl !== 216) continue;      // ← 只查了牆!
const n14 = () => 1 + rng.nextInt(3);
```
**缺 `!active()` 和 `!anyLava()` 兩個前置條件。**

定量推演(與你的兩個讀數精確吻合):
- 蜂巢 ~320×600 ≈ 19 萬格,牆 187/216 覆蓋幾乎全部;其中空氣格只佔 ~10-25%。
- **原版**:4200 次採樣只有命中"空氣+沙漠牆"才進窗口 → 約 50 次觸發,窄腔 4×4 窗口內平均只有 ~1.5 個可放置空氣格 → **~1.5 格/簇** ✓(正是你的金標讀數)。
- **我們**:幾乎每次採樣都進窗口 → 約 200+ 次觸發,窗口密集成片相連 → 合併成更長連通串 → **~3.9 格/簇** ✓(正是你的讀數)。
- 附帶傷害:每次多消耗 4 個 `genRand` 樣本 → **該 pass 之後整條隨機流位級分叉**,所有後續 pass 的擺放全部錯位(種子等價違約)。

**修法**(一行級,不要改其他):在 `GemPasses.ts:255` 牆檢查之前加
```ts
const ii = st.idx(x, y);
if (st.flags[ii]) continue;                                    // !active()
if (st.liquid[ii] > 0 && st.liquidType[ii] === 2) continue;    // !anyLava()
```
(同文件 `:243-244` 的"地下單格"循環已有這兩個檢查,照抄即可。)

**附帶結論**:`_gem-dist-audit.test.ts` 裡其實已經有獨立於琥珀的腔體度量(`:81-98` `desertWallAir`、`:123-175` `cavity()` 連通域直方圖 + `interiorRatio`)。**建議先修琥珀 bug 再重跑**,用 `cavity()` 的 `interiorRatio`/直方圖判斷腔體是否真的更開闊——而不是用琥珀格/簇。

## 其餘偏差(均為低影響,不影響腔體開闊度)

2. **`UpdateDesertHiveBounds` 缺失**(`DesertHive.cs:396-399` vs `DesertPass.ts:702-706`):原版把 `UndergroundDesertHiveLocation` 收縮到**實際雕刻範圍**;我們用靜態矩形。消費方(Corruption/BuriedChests/Mushroom/CaveHouses)避讓區偏大 → 只會更保守,不會更開闊。
3. **`AddTileVariance` 第二遍裝飾未放置**(`DesertHive.cs:474-496` vs `DesertPass.ts:688-694`):485 蟻卵/484 小石/165/187 化石只消耗擲骰不落格。擲骰順序已對齊(`Next(20)`→`Next(4)`→`Next(30)` 次序一致),純視覺缺失,非實心,不影響腔體。
4. **`CleanupArea` 幀號化省略**(`DesertBiome.cs:55-71`):僅 SquareWallFrame/TileFrame,無格數據變化,`caves-checkpoint.test.ts:102` 已註明。OK。
5. 若修完琥珀後 `cavity()` 仍顯示更開闊,再回頭查**上游**(蜂巢外環保留舊空氣,見 §1.3):先看 `caves-checkpoint.test.ts` 是否在 `desertmound` 或更早(沙丘/沙團/小洞/洞穴鏈)出現首個 ✗——`firstBad` 會直接指出分叉 pass。

# 4. while / 死循環審計(防死循環專項)

**`DesertPass.ts` 全文沒有任何 `while` 循環**(grep 證實)。唯一的無界循環:

| 位置 | 形態 | 硬上界? | 終止性分析 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `DesertPass.ts:148` `for (;;)`(沙漠放置重試) | 無顯式計數上界 | **有隱性確定上界** | 成功即 `break`(`:150`);否則 `tries++`,超過 `w/4` 換向(`:154-155`),`flips>=2` 後 `skipCheck=true`(`:157`)→ `:159-163` **強制 break**。最大迭代 ≈ `2*(w/4)+1` ≈ 2101 次(w=4200)。**安全** |
| `src/core/rng.ts:274` `FastRandom.nextMax` 拒絕採樣 `for (;;)` | 無計數上界 | 數學上必然終止 | 接受概率 `1-(max-1)/2^31`;DesertPass 只用 `max=3` → 拒絕概率 ~10⁻⁹。**且這是原版逐位算法,加計數上界會破壞位級對齊——不要動** |

歷史事故模式(`liquidType+1` 編碼照抄 `==0` 必死循環)在本文件**不存在**:所有 `=== 0` 硬幣(`:545, :562, :581, :612, :214, :221`)都在有界 `for` 內;`clearEverything`/`resetToType` 對 `liquidType` 一律顯式置 0(`:202, :206`),沒有 `+1` 自增編碼。

---

# 5. 建議修法(不動 DesertPass.ts)

1. **只改 `GemPasses.ts:251-258`**:補 `!active` + `!anyLava` 錨點檢查(§3 頭號偏差)。預期琥珀格/簇從 ~3.9 回落到 ~1.5,且修復後該 pass 之後的隨機流對齊。
2. 重跑 `npx vitest run tests/_gem-dist-audit.test.ts`,看 `desertWallAir` 與 `cavity()` 的 `interiorRatio`/直方圖是否金標與我們收斂——**這兩個才是腔體開闊度的乾淨度量**。
3. 同時確認 `npx vitest run tests/caves-checkpoint.test.ts` 的 `desertentrance/deserthive/desertdone` 三項(含 `:wall`)仍為 ✓——它是"蜂巢雕刻位級等價"的權威證據。
4. 若第 2 步仍顯示開闊,按 `firstBad` 順藤摸瓜查洞穴鏈上游(蜂巢外環保留舊空氣的機制決定了上游洞穴差異會直接透傳成沙漠腔體差異)。
